# Question Batch Map v2

This is the dependency-audited assignment of all 2,308 stable workbook question IDs to the GeoSurvey project sequence.

- P#### = logical project position
- Q#### = stable original workbook question ID
- Batch 000 = setup only, no interview questions
- Batches 001-461 = five questions each
- Batch 462 = three questions
- Batches 001-002 are frozen because they are already implemented
- Batches 003-462 were rebuilt after semantic prerequisite auditing

## Batch 001
Theme: JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
Project order: P0001-P0005  
Questions: Q0012, Q0013, Q0014, Q0015, Q0016

- **P0001 | Q0012** | Stage 01 | JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
  What is the role of the JVM in making Java platform-independent?
- **P0002 | Q0013** | Stage 01 | JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
  Can a machine have multiple versions of JDK or JRE installed?
- **P0003 | Q0014** | Stage 01 | JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
  Can you tell me what JVM is and how it works?
- **P0004 | Q0015** | Stage 01 | JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
  If you were explaining to new developers how Java code executes, how would you describe the role of JVM in running a simple Java program that prints Hello World?
- **P0005 | Q0016** | Stage 01 | JDK, JRE & JVM Overview  
  Can you tell me the difference between JDK, JRE and JVM?
